  if(!getCookie('websiteUsesCookies')){
    $('#cookie-notification').show();
    $cookieNotification = $('#cookie-notification');
    if( $cookieNotification.length > 0 ) {
      var cookieNotificationHeight = $cookieNotification.outerHeight();
      $cookieNotification.css({ bottom: '0px' });
      $('.cookie-accept').click( function(){
        $cookieNotification.css({ bottom: -cookieNotificationHeight });
        setCookie('websiteUsesCookies', 'yesConfirmed', 30);
        return false;
      });
    }
  }
});
function setCookie(name,value,days) {
    var expires = "";
    if (days) {
        var date = new Date();
        date.setTime(date.getTime() + (days*24*60*60*1000));
        expires = "; expires=" + date.toUTCString();
    }
    document.cookie = name + "=" + (value || "")  + expires + "; path=/";
}
function getCookie(name) {
    var nameEQ = name + "=";
    var ca = document.cookie.split(';');
    for(var i=0;i < ca.length;i++) {
        var c = ca[i];
        while (c.charAt(0)==' ') c = c.substring(1,c.length);
        if (c.indexOf(nameEQ) == 0) return c.substring(nameEQ.length,c.length);
    }
    return null;
}
function eraseCookie(name) {   
    document.cookie = name+'=; Max-Age=-99999999;';  
}
  function validaAltaNewsletter(IdForm, IdEmail, IdPolitica,IdComunicaciones) {
    var ok = false;
    if ($("#"+IdComunicaciones).prop('checked') == true) {
          if ($("#"+IdPolitica).prop('checked') == true) {
              if ($('#'+IdEmail).val() != "") {
                  ok = true;
              }
              else
              $.fancybox.open(jQuery.validator.messages.email);	
          }
          else
          $.fancybox.open("Debe aceptar la política de privacidad para poder suscribirse");
    }
    else
    $.fancybox.open("Debe aceptar el envio de comunicaciones comerciales de TomaTicket.");

    if (ok)
      addtonewsletter($('#'+IdEmail).val());
  }
	function addtonewsletter (email) {

		$.ajax({
			async: false,
			type: "POST",
			url: "xGuardaEmail.php",
			dataType: "json",
			data: "email="+email,
			beforeSend: function(objeto){
			},
			success: function (datos) {
				if (datos != false)
        $.fancybox.open('<p>Hemos recibido su e-mail. Le mantendremos informado con nuestros boletines.</p>');
				else
        $.fancybox.open('<p><strong>ERROR:</strong></p><p>El e-mail indicado no pudo ser enviado correctamente.</p>');
			}
		});
	}
	function removefromnewsletter (email) {

		$.ajax({
			async: false,
			type: "POST",
			url: "xBorraEmail.php",
			dataType: "json",
			data: "email="+email,
			beforeSend: function(objeto){
			},
			success: function (datos) {
				if (datos != false)
        $.fancybox.open('<p>Se ha dado de baja correctamente. Ya no recibirá más correos de TomaTicket.</p>');
				else
        $.fancybox.open('<p><strong>ERROR:</strong></p><p>El e-mail indicado no existe en nuestra base de datos.</p>');
			}
		});
	}

<script>
  // Guardar localstorage de términos de búsqueda usados y generar etiquetas con los mismos
  // frmBuscadorPrincipal
  var ultimasBusquedas;
  $(document).ready(()=>{

    if(localStorage.getItem("ultimasbusquedas") == null){
      localStorage.setItem("ultimasbusquedas", JSON.stringify([]));
    }
    ultimasBusquedas = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("ultimasbusquedas"));
    console.warn(ultimasBusquedas);
    // Crear etiquetas
    $('#contenedoretiquetas').empty();
    if(ultimasBusquedas.length > 0){
      $('#contenedoretiquetas').append('<div class="SearchTags"><span>Visto recientemente: </span></div>');
      for(let i=ultimasBusquedas.length-1;i>=0;i-- ){
        $('#contenedoretiquetas').append('<div id="tt-tag-'+i+'" class="tt-tag"></div>');
        $('#contenedoretiquetas .tt-tag:last').append('<span><a target=_blank class="link-tag" href="'+ultimasBusquedas[i].url+'">'+ultimasBusquedas[i].nombre+'</a><a onclick=cleararray("'+ultimasBusquedas[i].url+'","tt-tag-'+i+'") href="#!" class="icon-line-circle-cross inline-block link-icon"></a></span>');
      }
      //$('#contenedoretiquetas').append('<div class="tt-tag"></div>');
      $('#contenedoretiquetas').append('<a onclick=fulminateall() class="link-clean-search" href="#!">Borrar todas las búsquedas </a>');
    }
  })
  function cleararray(URL,eleid){
    if(URL == "all"){
      $('#contenedoretiquetas div').remove();
      localStorage.setItem("ultimasbusquedas", JSON.stringify(null));
    } else {
      for(let i in ultimasBusquedas){
        if(ultimasBusquedas[i].url == URL){
          ultimasBusquedas.splice(i, 1);
        }
      }
      $('#'+eleid).remove();
      localStorage.setItem("ultimasbusquedas", JSON.stringify(ultimasBusquedas));
    }
  }
  function fulminateall(){
    $('#contenedoretiquetas').empty();
    localStorage.setItem("ultimasbusquedas", JSON.stringify([]));
  }
  
</script>

<script>
        $('.ui.search') .search({
            searchOnFocus: true,
            apiSettings: {
             
              method: 'GET',
              beforeSend: function(settings) {
               
               //console.log('antes de enviar');
              var URl=document.getElementById('miBusqueda').value;
          
               if(sessionStorage.getItem(URl)!=undefined)
                  settings.response=JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem(URl));
                 // sessionStorage.clear();
               return settings;
             },
              onResponse : function(Reponse) {
                var URl=document.getElementById('miBusqueda').value;

                sessionStorage.setItem(URl,JSON.stringify(Reponse)); //ó sessionStorage[producto]=precio
                //sessionStorage.clear();
                return Reponse;

              },
              url: 'https://www.tomaticket.es/ajax/xSearcher1.php?text={query}'
            },
            type: 'category',
            minCharacters: 3
          
          })
        ;
        $('.ui.dropdown').dropdown({
          clearable: true, 
          forceSelection: false
        });

       
        

       


       
    </script>

<script>
jQuery(document).ready(function($) {

    $(document).click(function(event) {
      if(event.target.id!='miBusqueda'){
        $('#results2').hide();
        $("#results2").attr('class', 'results');
      }
       
    });
    $('#miBusqueda').click(function() {
      if($('#miBusqueda').val()!=''){
        $('#results2').show();
        $("#results2").attr('class', 'results visible transition');
      }
    });


    $('#primary-menu-trigger,#overlay-menu-close').click(function() {
      if( $('#primary-menu').find('ul.mobile-primary-menu').length > 0 ) {
        $( '#primary-menu > ul.mobile-primary-menu, #primary-menu > div > ul.mobile-primary-menu' ).toggleClass("show");
      } else {
        $( '#primary-menu > ul, #primary-menu > div > ul' ).toggleClass("show");
      }
      $body.toggleClass("primary-menu-open");
      return false;
    });


    var swiperElement = $('.swiper_wrapper'),
    slideNumberTotal = swiperElement.find('#slide-number-total'),
    slideNumberCurrent = swiperElement.find('#slide-number-current'),
    sliderVideoAutoPlay = swiperElement.attr('data-video-autoplay');

    var mySwiper = new Swiper ('.swiper-container', {
      // Optional parameters
      direction: 'horizontal',
      loop: 'true',
      speed:600,
      autoplay: true,
      autoplay: {
        delay: 5000,
      },
      slidesPerView: 1,
      effect: 'slide',
      navigation: {
        nextEl: '#slider-arrow-right',
        prevEl: '#slider-arrow-left',
      },
      on: {
          slideChangeTransitionStart: function(swiper){
            if( slideNumberCurrent.length > 0 ){
              if( elementLoop == true ) {
                slideNumberCurrent.html( Number( swiperElement.find('.swiper-slide.swiper-slide-active').attr('data-swiper-slide-index') ) + 1 );
              } else {
                slideNumberCurrent.html( swiperSlider.activeIndex + 1 );
              }
            }
            $('[data-caption-animate]').each(function(){
              var $toAnimateElement = $(this),
                elementAnimation = $toAnimateElement.attr('data-caption-animate');
              if( $toAnimateElement.parents('.swiper-slide').hasClass('swiper-slide-active') ) { return true; }
              $toAnimateElement.removeClass('animated').removeClass(elementAnimation).addClass('not-animated');
            });
          },
          slideChangeTransitionEnd: function(swiper){
            swiperElement.find('.swiper-slide').each(function(){
              var slideEl = $(this);
              if( slideEl.find('video').length > 0 && sliderVideoAutoPlay == true ) { slideEl.find('video').get(0).pause(); }
              if( slideEl.find('.yt-bg-player.mb_YTPlayer:not(.customjs)').length > 0 ) { slideEl.find('.yt-bg-player.mb_YTPlayer:not(.customjs)').YTPPause(); }
            });
            swiperElement.find('.swiper-slide:not(".swiper-slide-active")').each(function(){
              var slideEl = $(this);
              if( slideEl.find('video').length > 0 ) {
                if( slideEl.find('video').get(0).currentTime != 0 ) { slideEl.find('video').get(0).currentTime = 0; }
              }
              if( slideEl.find('.yt-bg-player.mb_YTPlayer:not(.customjs)').length > 0 ) {
                slideEl.find('.yt-bg-player.mb_YTPlayer:not(.customjs)').YTPGetPlayer().seekTo( slideEl.find('.yt-bg-player.mb_YTPlayer:not(.customjs)').attr('data-start') );
              }
            });
            if( swiperElement.find('.swiper-slide.swiper-slide-active').find('video').length > 0 && sliderVideoAutoPlay == true ) { swiperElement.find('.swiper-slide.swiper-slide-active').find('video').get(0).play(); }
            if( swiperElement.find('.swiper-slide.swiper-slide-active').find('.yt-bg-player.mb_YTPlayer:not(.customjs)').length > 0 && sliderVideoAutoPlay == true ) { swiperElement.find('.swiper-slide.swiper-slide-active').find('.yt-bg-player.mb_YTPlayer:not(.customjs)').YTPPlay(); }

            swiperElement.find('.swiper-slide.swiper-slide-active [data-caption-animate]').each(function(){
              var $toAnimateElement = $(this),
                toAnimateDelay = $toAnimateElement.attr('data-caption-delay'),
                toAnimateDelayTime = 0;
              if( toAnimateDelay ) { toAnimateDelayTime = Number( toAnimateDelay ) + 300; } else { toAnimateDelayTime = 300; }
              if( !$toAnimateElement.hasClass('animated') ) {
                $toAnimateElement.addClass('not-animated');
                var elementAnimation = $toAnimateElement.attr('data-caption-animate');
                setTimeout(function() {
                  $toAnimateElement.removeClass('not-animated').addClass( elementAnimation + ' animated');
                }, toAnimateDelayTime);
              }
            });
          }

      }

    });


    $("#movilCarouselFeatured, #movilCarousel, #movilCarouselSorteos").each(function() {
      $(this).on('initialize.owl.carousel initialized.owl.carousel ' +
        'initialize.owl.carousel initialize.owl.carousel ' +
        'resize.owl.carousel resized.owl.carousel ' +
        'refresh.owl.carousel refreshed.owl.carousel ' +
        'update.owl.carousel updated.owl.carousel ' +
        'drag.owl.carousel dragged.owl.carousel ' +
        'translate.owl.carousel translated.owl.carousel ' +
        'to.owl.carousel changed.owl.carousel',
        function(e) {
          $('.' + e.type)
            .removeClass('secondary')
            .addClass('success');
          window.setTimeout(function() {
            $('.' + e.type)
              .removeClass('success')
              .addClass('secondary');
          }, 500);
        });
      $(this).owlCarousel({
        loop: true,
        nav: true,
        lazyLoad: true,
        margin: 20,
        video: true,
        navText: [
          '<i class="icon-angle-left"></i>',
          '<i class="icon-angle-right"></i>'
        ],
        responsive: {
          0: {
            items: 1
          },
          600: {
            items: 2
          },
          800: {
            items: 3
          },
          960: {
            items: 4,
          },
          1200: {
            items: 4
          }
        }
      });
    });


    $('#main-menu').slicknav({
      prependTo:'#menuResponsiveContenedor',
      brand:'<a class="logo" href="https://www.tomaticket.es/"><img  src="//static.tomaticket.es/img/logo-tomaticket.png"  alt="Ir a Inicio" style="margin:20px 0; max-width: 225px; width: 100%;" /></a>'
    });

    var pagActual = 1;
    var pagMax = 4;
    $('#btnMasEventos').on("click", function (e) {
      if (pagActual < pagMax) {
        pagActual += 1;
        $(".pagina"+pagActual).fadeIn(500);        
      }
      if (pagActual == pagMax) {
        $(this).parent().hide()
        $("#infoMasEventos").show();
        
      }
      e.preventDefault();
    });

});


</script>
